🧭 Planning & ReasoningPlanning & Reasoning

· 发展脉络· Development

该方向致力于解决具身智能体在开放动态环境中进行长视界规划与因果推理的难题，核心演进路径是从依赖静态预训练数据的模仿学习，转向基于世界模型（World Models）的主动预测与试错规划。早期研究侧重于将视觉语言模型（VLM）与基础策略结合，解决空间理解与语义对齐问题；中期发展出世界动作模型（WAM）与联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），实现了在潜在空间中进行高效、可解释的推演。近期趋势聚焦于物理一致性、多模态触觉融合、分层思维链推理以及安全验证机制，旨在构建具备物理常识、能处理长程任务且具备自我进化能力的具身大脑。This direction is dedicated to solving the challenges of long-horizon planning and causal reasoning for embodied agents in open dynamic environments. The core evolutionary path shifts from imitation learning dependent on static pre-trained data to active prediction and trial-and-error planning based on World Models. Early research focused on combining Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with foundational policies to address spatial understanding and semantic alignment; mid-stage development produced World Action Models (WAMs) and Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA), enabling efficient and interpretable reasoning in latent spaces. Recent trends focus on physical consistency, multi-modal tactile fusion, hierarchical chain-of-thought reasoning, and safety verification mechanisms, aiming to construct an embodied brain with physical common sense, capable of handling long-range tasks and possessing self-evolution capabilities.

切入角度Perspectives

  • 思维链与显式推理增强Chain-of-Thought and Explicit Reasoning Enhancement
  • 分层与模块化架构Hierarchical and modular architecture
  • 世界模型与隐式规划World Models and Implicit Planning
  • 空间感知与物理常识Spatial Perception and Physical Common Sense
  • 数据驱动与仿真基准Data-driven and Simulation Benchmarks
  • 系统架构与理论框架System Architecture and Theoretical Framework

· 各角度时间线· Timeline per Perspective

思维链与显式推理增强Chain-of-Thought and Explicit Reasoning Enhancement

该方向旨在解决具身智能体在复杂环境中‘盲目行动’与‘泛化性差’的痛点，核心思路是通过引入显式的中间推理步骤（如视觉草图、语言思维链、物理可行性检查），强制模型在生成动作前进行‘慢思考’。演进脉络从早期的文本/视觉CoT辅助（如#9, #11），发展到针对机器人特性的ECoT与潜规划（#15, #42），再到引入自我反思、物理约束及神经符号方法（#16, #56, #75）。尽管显著提升了长程规划成功率与鲁棒性，但普遍面临推理延迟高、计算成本大及‘思考’是否真正理解物理本质的质疑，正从单纯的数据驱动向‘思考-验证’闭环及神经符号融合方向演进。This direction aims to address the pain points of 'blind action' and 'poor generalization' in embodied agents within complex environments. The core approach involves introducing explicit intermediate reasoning steps (such as visual sketches, language CoT, and physical feasibility checks) to force the model to engage in 'slow thinking' before generating actions. The evolutionary trajectory has progressed from early text/visual CoT assistance (e.g., #9, #11), to ECoT and latent planning tailored for robotics (#15, #42), and further to incorporating self-reflection, physical constraints, and neuro-symbolic methods (#16, #56, #75). Although significantly improving long-horizon planning success rates and robustness, it universally faces challenges such as high reasoning latency, substantial computational costs, and skepticism regarding whether the 'thinking' truly grasps the physical essence. It is evolving from purely data-driven approaches towards a 'think-verify' closed loop and neuro-symbolic fusion.

2026-09
World-Coherent Decoding: Self-Verifying Test-Time Planning for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对世界动作模型（WAM）在测试时生成大量低质量或物理不一致候选动作，导致盲目采样效率低下的痛点。Addressing the pain point that World Action Models (WAM) generate a large number of low-quality or physically inconsistent candidate actions during testing, leading to inefficient blind sampling.
方法: Method: 提出世界一致性解码（WCD）策略，利用生成过程中的内部信号（如惊讶度、路径努力）在开弓前对候选动作进行自我验证与打分。Proposing a World Consistency Decoding (WCD) strategy that utilizes internal signals during the generation process (such as surprise and path effort) to self-verify and score candidate actions before execution.
推进: Advance: 将‘事后验证’转化为‘事前预判’，证明了在冻结骨干模型前提下，通过智能推理策略而非暴力堆算力，可显著提升长视界任务的成功率。Transforming 'post-hoc verification' into 'pre-hoc prediction', proving that under the premise of a frozen backbone model, intelligent reasoning strategies rather than brute-force computing power can significantly improve success rates in long-horizon tasks.
2026-09
VeriPhy: Agentic Physical Reasoning for World Model Evaluation and Refinement
动机: Motivation: 针对具身智能体在复杂物理交互中缺乏可验证性与可追溯性的问题，旨在解决盲目行动导致的物理一致性缺失。Aiming to address the lack of verifiability and traceability in embodied agents during complex physical interactions, thereby solving the absence of physical consistency caused by blind actions.
方法: Method: 构建‘先计划后执行’的严谨链条，将物理义务编译为类型化计划，并利用冻结专家模型进行执行与证据回溯。Constructing a rigorous 'plan-then-execute' chain, compiling physical obligations into typed plans, and utilizing frozen expert models for execution and evidence backtracking.
推进: Advance: 首次实现了物理推理过程的完全可审计化，证明了通过解耦仿真控制与生成，可在不提升召回率的情况下显著增强系统的可解释性与安全性。Achieving full auditability of the physical reasoning process for the first time, proving that decoupling simulation control from generation can significantly enhance system interpretability and safety without improving recall rates.
2026-09
HINT: Human-Intent Inception for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为了解决长程任务中模型因高频推理导致的注意力分散及计算资源浪费，试图在保持性能的同时降低推理频率。Attempting to reduce inference frequency while maintaining performance to address the attention dispersion and computational resource waste caused by high-frequency inference in models for long-horizon tasks.
方法: Method: 引入外部人类意图提示头（Human-Intent Inception），通过预定义的四种操作模式路由机制，强制模型在关键节点进行显式意图对齐。Introducing an external Human-Intent Inception head, utilizing a pre-defined four-operation mode routing mechanism to force the model to perform explicit intent alignment at critical nodes.
推进: Advance: 以零参数训练成本实现了长程操作中的‘慢思考’机制，证明了通过工程化的外部提示而非模型微调，即可有效抑制长视界任务中的注意力漂移。Achieving a 'slow thinking' mechanism in long-horizon operations with zero-parameter training costs, proving that engineered external prompts rather than model fine-tuning can effectively suppress attention drift in long-horizon tasks.
2026-09
LookStep: Efficient Vision-Language Navigation with Linguistic Foresight and Event Driven Memory
动机: Motivation: 针对现有视觉导航方法过度依赖昂贵的3D重建或外部空间工具，导致边缘设备部署困难的问题。Addressing the problem that existing visual navigation methods overly rely on expensive 3D reconstruction or external spatial tools, leading to difficulties in edge device deployment.
方法: Method: 提出语言先验与事件驱动机制，将‘下一步动作’转化为对‘未来状态’的语言评估，利用语言模型内部能力替代显式空间建模。Proposing a language prior and event-driven mechanism that transforms 'next action' into a linguistic evaluation of 'future states', utilizing the internal capabilities of language models to replace explicit spatial modeling.
推进: Advance: 在单目RGB输入下实现了高效的长程规划，证明了利用语言标签替代高成本空间建模可在显著降低内存占用的同时保持导航性能。Achieving efficient long-horizon planning under monocular RGB input, proving that using language labels to replace high-cost spatial modeling can maintain navigation performance while significantly reducing memory usage.
2026-09
CAD-Based Relation Learning and Geometric-Symbolic Planning for Robotic Assembly
动机: Motivation: 针对具身智能体在复杂装配任务中因纯数据驱动导致的泛化性差及缺乏物理可解释性痛点，旨在解决非完美感知下的规划失效问题。Aiming to address the pain points of poor generalization and lack of physical interpretability in embodied agents for complex assembly tasks caused by purely data-driven approaches, and to solve the problem of planning failure under imperfect perception.
方法: Method: 提出GLARE框架，将深度学习提取的语义关系与符号几何规划相结合，并引入人机回环机制以在规划失败时进行显式修正。Proposes the GLARE framework, which combines semantic relationships extracted by deep learning with symbolic geometric planning, and introduces a human-in-the-loop mechanism to perform explicit corrections when planning fails.
推进: Advance: 实现了从黑盒端到端向神经符号混合推理的演进，显著提升了在不完美的CAD数据环境下的规划鲁棒性与可解释性。Achieves an evolution from black-box end-to-end to neuro-symbolic hybrid reasoning, significantly enhancing planning robustness and interpretability in environments with imperfect CAD data.
2026-09
DriveMCP: An Agentic AI framework for Advanced Driver Assistance System
动机: Motivation: 为了解决大模型在自动驾驶中“盲目行动”及难以动态适应法规约束的问题，需将隐性推理显式化为合规性检查与物理护栏。To address the issues of 'blind action' and difficulty in dynamically adapting to regulatory constraints in large models for autonomous driving, it is necessary to make implicit reasoning explicit as compliance checks and physical guardrails.
方法: Method: 构建DriveMCP框架，利用MCP协议集成规则引擎与RAG技术，强制模型在决策前进行法条检索与RSS物理约束验证。Constructs the DriveMCP framework, utilizing the MCP protocol to integrate rule engines and RAG technology, forcing the model to perform legal retrieval and RSS physical constraint verification before decision-making.
推进: Advance: 将推理过程从单纯的数据拟合扩展为“感知-检索-验证”的闭环，证明了显式引入外部规则能显著提升长程规划的安全边界。Expands the reasoning process from simple data fitting to a 'perception-retrieval-verification' closed loop, proving that explicitly introducing external rules can significantly enhance the safety boundaries of long-horizon planning.
2026-09
ManiSkillFormer: Demonstration-Free Compositional Manipulation via Task-Conditioned Geometric Contracts
动机: Motivation: 针对纯视觉-动作模型在长程任务中缺乏逻辑连贯性及泛化能力弱的问题，试图通过显式的几何约束替代端到端的黑盒映射。Aiming to address the lack of logical coherence and weak generalization capabilities of pure vision-action models in long-horizon tasks, it attempts to replace end-to-end black-box mappings with explicit geometric constraints.
方法: Method: 提出ManiSkillFormer，利用LLM生成任务条件的“几何合同”，将感知、推理与技能库解耦，强制执行基于语义约束的中间步骤。Proposes ManiSkillFormer, which utilizes LLMs to generate 'geometric contracts' for task conditions, decoupling perception, reasoning, and skill libraries, and enforcing intermediate steps based on semantic constraints.
推进: Advance: 验证了“合同制”推理在无需演示情况下的有效性，通过显式几何约束解决了感知-动作接口的模糊性，提升了长程任务的生存率。Validates the effectiveness of 'contract-based' reasoning without demonstrations, resolving the ambiguity of the perception-action interface through explicit geometric constraints and improving the survival rate of long-horizon tasks.
2026-09
Few-Shot Demonstrations Elicit the Use of In-Context World Representations in LLMs
动机: Motivation: 揭示LLM虽能隐式构建世界模型，却缺乏将其显式用于规划决策的机制，导致‘有图无路’的盲目行动问题。Revealing that while LLMs can implicitly construct World Models, they lack mechanisms to explicitly utilize them for planning and decision-making, leading to the 'map without a path' problem of blind action.
方法: Method: 利用少样本演示（Few-Shot Demonstrations）作为干预手段，强制激活并引导模型读取其内部表征中的世界结构信息。Utilizing Few-Shot Demonstrations as an intervention to forcibly activate and guide the model to read world structure information from its internal representations.
推进: Advance: 证明了表征的几何位置而非质量是决定推理能力的关键，挑战了‘构造即使用’的假设，为显式推理提供了新的表征学习视角。Demonstrating that the geometric position of representations, rather than their quality, is the key determinant of reasoning capability, challenging the 'construct then use' assumption and providing a new representation learning perspective for explicit reasoning.
2026-09
Taming CoT Obfuscation in VLMs: From Mechanistic Evidence to Activation Enforcement
动机: Motivation: 针对RL训练后VLM中CoT退化为无意义模板堆砌的现象，解决推理过程‘黑盒化’导致无法验证物理可行性的痛点。Addressing the phenomenon where CoT in VLMs degrades into meaningless template stacking after RL training, solving the pain point of the 'black-box' reasoning process that prevents verification of physical feasibility.
方法: Method: 采用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从机制层面解剖模型，通过激活强制（Activation Enforcement）技术约束思维链的生成路径。Adopting Sparse Autoencoders (SAE) to dissect models at the mechanistic level, constraining the generation path of Chain-of-Thought through Activation Enforcement techniques.
推进: Advance: 首次从表征层面证实并干预了CoT的‘伪装’行为，在恢复推理可解释性的同时，为构建可验证的显式推理闭环提供了硬核技术路径。For the first time, confirming and intervening in the 'disguise' behavior of CoT at the representation level, restoring reasoning interpretability while providing a hard-core technical path for building verifiable explicit reasoning loops.
2024-06
Visual Sketchpad: Sketching as a Visual Chain of Thought for Multimodal Language Models
动机: Motivation: 为了解决纯文本CoT在视觉任务中表达受限的问题，试图让模型像人一样通过‘画图’进行视觉推理。Aims to address the limited expression of pure text CoT in visual tasks, attempting to enable models to perform visual reasoning by 'drawing' like humans.
方法: Method: 引入‘电子画板’机制，让多模态模型生成辅助线、热力图等视觉草图作为中间推理步骤。Introduces an 'electronic whiteboard' mechanism, allowing multimodal models to generate visual sketches such as auxiliary lines and heatmaps as intermediate reasoning steps.
推进: Advance: 将视觉思维链从文本拉至像素级，显著提升了数学与视觉任务表现，但依赖外部工具且推理效率低。Shifts visual CoT from text to the pixel level, significantly improving performance on mathematical and visual tasks, but relies on external tools and suffers from low reasoning efficiency.
2024-07
Robotic Control via Embodied Chain-of-Thought Reasoning
动机: Motivation: 针对机器人直接端到端决策泛化性差的问题，试图给VLA模型强行增加‘看仔细再动手’的思考环节。Addresses the poor generalization of direct end-to-end decision-making in robots, attempting to forcibly add a 'look carefully before acting' thinking step to VLA models.
方法: Method: 提出ECoT框架，强制模型在动作生成前输出坐标、动作等结构化推理信息，并使用合成数据训练。Proposes the ECoT framework, forcing the model to output structured reasoning information such as coordinates and actions before generating actions, and utilizes synthetic data for training.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了OpenVLA在复杂任务上的成功率，但推理速度大幅下降且思考结构固定，缺乏动态调整能力。Significantly improves the success rate of OpenVLA on complex tasks, but causes a substantial drop in reasoning speed and features a fixed thinking structure lacking dynamic adjustment capabilities.
2025-03
CoT-VLA: Visual Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 为了解决VLA直接输出动作导致的规划模糊问题，试图用‘视觉脑补’生成中间目标图来辅助决策。Aims to address the planning ambiguity caused by VLA directly outputting actions, attempting to use 'visual imagination' to generate intermediate target images to assist decision-making.
方法: Method: 提出CoT-VLA，让模型先生成目标子图作为视觉思维链，再基于此规划动作。Proposes CoT-VLA, enabling the model to first generate target sub-images as a visual CoT, and then plan actions based on them.
推进: Advance: 在多指令任务中显著提升成功率，但生成图片步骤导致推理速度骤降，且图片质量不如专用扩散模型。Significantly improves success rates in multi-instruction tasks, but the image generation step causes a sharp drop in reasoning speed, and image quality is inferior to dedicated diffusion models.
2025-06
Edit Flows: Flow Matching with Edit Operations
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决非自回归序列生成中因固定位置掩码导致的“僵硬”问题，试图通过更灵活的编辑操作增强模型在规划生成中的适应性。Aiming to solve the 'rigidity' problem caused by fixed position masking in non-autoregressive sequence generation, it attempts to enhance the model's adaptability in planning generation through more flexible editing operations.
方法: Method: 提出 Edit Flows，利用插入、删除、替换三种离散编辑操作构建流匹配过程，替代传统的 token 位置掩码机制。Proposes Edit Flows, utilizing insertion, deletion, and replacement as three discrete editing operations to construct a flow matching process, replacing the traditional token position masking mechanism.
推进: Advance: 证明了编辑操作比掩码更灵活，在代码生成等任务上显著超越 Mask DFM，为离散流匹配提供了新的生成范式。Demonstrates that editing operations are more flexible than masking, significantly outperforming Mask DFM on tasks like code generation, providing a new generative paradigm for discrete flow matching.
2025-11
DeepThinkVLA: Enhancing Reasoning Capability of Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对强行自回归生成CoT导致性能下降及SFT无法泛化的问题，试图通过RL对齐让CoT真正指导决策。Addresses the performance degradation caused by forcibly autoregressive generation of CoT and the inability of SFT to generalize, attempting to align CoT with decision-making through RL.
方法: Method: 提出DeepThinkVLA，采用混合注意力与两阶段训练，利用RL奖励将CoT从‘废话’转化为有效规划。Proposes DeepThinkVLA, adopting mixed attention and two-stage training, using RL rewards to transform CoT from 'nonsense' into effective planning.
推进: Advance: 在LIBERO基准上达到97%成功率，证明了‘想清楚再动手’的有效性，但依赖云大模型生成数据成本高昂。Achieves a 97% success rate on the LIBERO benchmark, proving the effectiveness of 'thinking clearly before acting', but relies on cloud-based large models for data generation, which is costly.
2026-06
Adapting Generalist Robot Policies with Semantic Reinforcement Learning
动机: Motivation: 为了解决长程任务中动作空间搜索困难的问题，试图将语言提示作为动作进行强化学习。Aims to address the difficulty of action space search in long-horizon tasks by attempting to use language prompts as actions for reinforcement learning.
方法: Method: 提出语义强化学习，把VLM的语言提示直接作为动作空间，挖掘预训练模型中的技能库。Proposes semantic reinforcement learning, treating VLM language prompts directly as the action space to mine skill libraries from pre-trained models.
推进: Advance: 实现了长程任务的新解法，但本质是调参而非创造新技能，且VLM推理慢影响实时性。Realizes a new solution for long-horizon tasks, but essentially involves parameter tuning rather than creating new skills, and the slow VLM reasoning affects real-time performance.
2026-06
E-TTS: A New Embodied Test-Time Scaling Framework for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为了解决具身TTS缺乏推理验证导致盲目采样的问题，试图将推理与动作绑定打分并引入记忆。Aims to address the problem of blind sampling caused by the lack of reasoning verification in embodied TTS, attempting to bind reasoning with action scoring and introduce memory.
方法: Method: 提出E-TTS框架，结合历史记忆缓冲，利用大模型作为推理验证器对动作进行闭环评估。Proposes the E-TTS framework, combining historical memory buffers and utilizing large models as reasoning verifiers to perform closed-loop evaluation of actions.
推进: Advance: 在仿真和真机上显著提升成功率，但验证器延迟高，难以在低算力边缘设备部署。Significantly improves success rates in both simulation and real-world settings, but the high latency of the verifier makes deployment on low-compute edge devices difficult.
2026-06
PhysReflect-VLA: Physical Feasibility and Self-Reflective Regulation for Reliable Vision-Language-Action Policies
动机: Motivation: 针对VLA输出动作不考虑物理可行性导致失败的问题，试图给模型加上‘物理眼镜’和‘反思大脑’。Addresses the issue of failure caused by VLA outputting actions without considering physical feasibility, attempting to equip the model with 'physical glasses' and a 'reflective brain'.
方法: Method: 提出PhysReflect-VLA，外挂物理可行性评估模块和自反思模块，形成‘思考-验证’闭环。Proposes PhysReflect-VLA, adding an external physical feasibility assessment module and a self-reflection module to form a 'think-verify' closed loop.
推进: Advance: 在长程接触任务中显著提升了可靠性，但增加了计算开销，在高速动态场景下可能成为瓶颈。Significantly improves reliability in long-horizon contact tasks, but increases computational overhead and may become a bottleneck in high-speed dynamic scenarios.
2026-06
Einstein World Models
动机: Motivation: 试图让LLM像物理学家一样进行思想实验，将视频生成作为CoT的可视化中间假设。Attempts to enable LLMs to conduct thought experiments like physicists, using video generation as a visualized intermediate hypothesis for CoT.
方法: Method: 提出Einstein World Models，调用视频生成器生成rollout作为可检查的中间步骤。Proposes Einstein World Models, calling a video generator to produce rollouts as checkable intermediate steps.
推进: Advance: 将黑盒推理变为可检查的可视化过程，但依赖视频生成器的物理一致性，易产生幻觉。Transforms black-box reasoning into a checkable visualized process, but relies on the physical consistency of the video generator and is prone to hallucinations.
2026-06
TimeProVe: Propose, then Verify for Efficient Long Video Temporal Reasoning in Activities of Daily Living
动机: Motivation: 为了解决长视频推理计算成本过高的问题，试图通过‘先猜后验’机制压缩计算压力。Aims to address the excessively high computational costs of long-video reasoning by attempting to compress computational pressure through a 'guess-then-verify' mechanism.
方法: Method: 提出TimeProVe，用轻量级检测器压缩视频，仅对候选片段调用大VLM进行验证。Proposes TimeProVe, which compresses videos using a lightweight detector and calls a large VLM only for verification of candidate segments.
推进: Advance: 节省了93%成本并提升推理效率，但依赖动作检测器的准确性，漏检关键帧会导致推理失败。Reduces costs by 93% and improves inference efficiency, but relies on the accuracy of the action detector; missing key frames leads to inference failure.
2026-06
Can In-Context Learning Support Intrinsic Curiosity?
动机: Motivation: 试图验证ICL能否替代昂贵梯度更新来驱动内在好奇心，探索其理论边界。Attempts to verify whether ICL can replace expensive gradient updates to drive intrinsic curiosity, exploring its theoretical boundaries.
方法: Method: 通过理论证明和实验，分析ICL在通用MDP中估计贝叶斯信息增益的可行性与局限性。Through theoretical proof and experiments, analyzes the feasibility and limitations of ICL estimating Bayesian information gain in general MDPs.

方法: Method: 提出DreamLedger机制，为每个预测生成可审计的“信用文件”，通过事后结算与按需记账来动态调整规划器的信任度与计算资源分配。Proposing the DreamLedger mechanism, which generates auditable 'credit documents' for each prediction, dynamically adjusting the planner's trust level and computational resource allocation through post-settlement and on-demand bookkeeping.
推进: Advance: 实现了规划器消耗预测粒度的显式量化与优化，减少了无效想象，但指出该方案难以跨部署点迁移，属于典型的本地化验证策略。Achieving explicit quantification and optimization of the planner's consumption of prediction granularity, reducing ineffective imagination, but noting that this solution is difficult to migrate across deployment points, representing a typical localized validation strategy.

动机: Motivation: 解决传统强化学习策略“死脑筋”、缺乏自适应计算深度，无法像人类一样根据任务难度灵活调整思考深度的问题。It solves the problem of traditional reinforcement learning policies being 'stubborn' and lacking adaptive computational depth, unable to flexibly adjust thinking depth according to task difficulty like humans.
方法: Method: 提出Looped Actor架构，利用深度循环推理机制，让模型通过参数迭代反复“思考”以生成动作，而非单次前向传播。It proposes the Looped Actor architecture, utilizing a deep recursive reasoning mechanism to allow the model to repeatedly 'think' via parameter iteration to generate actions, rather than performing a single forward pass.
推进: Advance: 证明了深度循环推理能以极小参数量（1/16）在多步规划任务中超越大模型基线，实现了类似人类“灵光一闪”的自适应计算效率。It proves that deep recursive reasoning can surpass large model baselines in multi-step planning tasks with minimal parameters (1/16), achieving adaptive computational efficiency similar to human 'insights'.

分层与模块化架构Hierarchical and modular architecture

该方向核心在于通过分层与模块化架构解耦高层语义规划与底层动作执行，以平衡大模型的泛化能力与实时控制的安全性。早期工作如RT-H和CoPa尝试引入语言中间层或空间约束作为桥梁，但面临延迟高或3D推理弱的瓶颈。演进至PLUTO、ReKep及Hi Robot，研究转向混合架构与神经符号结合，利用求解器或行为树增强可解释性与鲁棒性。近期如OmniAct、TypeGo及DSWAM进一步引入异步双系统、OS级调度及多尺度世界模型，旨在解决长程任务中的记忆丢失与实时性矛盾，从单纯的任务拆解走向系统级的认知架构重构。The core of this direction lies in decoupling high-level semantic planning from low-level action execution through hierarchical and modular architectures to balance the generalization of large models with the safety of real-time control. Early works like RT-H and CoPa attempted to introduce language intermediate layers or spatial constraints as bridges but faced bottlenecks of high latency or weak 3D reasoning. Evolution to PLUTO, ReKep, and Hi Robot shifted research toward hybrid architectures and neuro-symbolic combinations, utilizing solvers or behavior trees to enhance interpretability and robustness. Recent works like OmniAct, TypeGo, and DSWAM further introduced asynchronous dual-systems, OS-level scheduling, and multi-scale world models, aiming to resolve the contradiction between memory loss and real-time performance in long-horizon tasks, moving from simple task decomposition to system-level cognitive architecture reconstruction.

世界模型与隐式规划World Models and Implicit Planning

本方向聚焦于利用世界模型实现隐式规划，核心思路是摒弃显式规则与奖励函数，转而通过自监督学习或大模型先验构建环境动态的隐式表征。演进脉络从早期依赖预训练视觉特征（如DINOv2）的零样本规划，发展到利用LLM隐式知识进行Web代理的“脑补”规划。近期研究进一步探索JEPA架构的稳定性与多模态预测（如LeWorldModel, V-JEPA 2），并尝试将视频生成模型直接转化为控制策略（Cosmos Policy）。同时，领域正从单纯追求预测精度转向解决多模态坍塌、物理一致性验证及理论泛化边界问题，旨在构建无需显式试错即可在复杂物理环境中进行长视野推理的通用具身智能体。This direction focuses on utilizing world models to achieve implicit planning; the core idea is to abandon explicit rules and reward functions, instead constructing implicit representations of environmental dynamics through self-supervised learning or large model priors. The evolutionary trajectory has progressed from early zero-shot planning relying on pre-trained visual features (e.g., DINOv2) to leveraging LLM implicit knowledge for 'mental simulation' planning in web agents. Recent research further explores the stability and multi-modal prediction of JEPA architectures (e.g., LeWorldModel, V-JEPA 2), and attempts to directly convert video generation models into control policies (Cosmos Policy). Meanwhile, the field is shifting from solely pursuing prediction accuracy to addressing multi-modal collapse, physical consistency verification, and theoretical generalization boundaries, aiming to build general embodied agents capable of long-horizon reasoning in complex physical environments without explicit trial-and-error.

Semantic Bayesian World Models
动机: Motivation: 指出当前架构中知识图谱的布尔逻辑与大模型的概率特性难以兼容，导致世界模型缺乏对不确定性的统一表征。Pointing out that Boolean logic in knowledge graphs and the probabilistic nature of large models are difficult to reconcile in current architectures, leading to a lack of unified representation of uncertainty in world models.
方法: Method: 提出语义贝叶斯世界模型（SBWM），利用贝叶斯网络将语义知识与概率推理统一，主张概率依附于稳定标识符而非字符串。Proposing a Semantic Bayesian World Model (SBWM) that utilizes Bayesian networks to unify semantic knowledge with probabilistic reasoning, advocating that probability attaches to stable identifiers rather than strings.
推进: Advance: 从理论层面构建了神经符号与贝叶斯推理融合的框架，为解决具身智能中语义与不确定性的根本冲突提供了新的范式，尽管工程落地尚需验证。Constructing a framework for the fusion of neuro-symbolic and Bayesian reasoning from a theoretical perspective, providing a new paradigm for resolving the fundamental conflict between semantics and uncertainty in embodied AI, although engineering implementation still requires verification.

Rethinking World Models for Safety-Critical Embodied Systems

SV-WAM: An Efficient Surround-View World-Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 解决全视角世界模型在实时自动驾驶中面临的“既要高感知精度又要低推理延迟”的工程矛盾。Addressing the engineering contradiction of 'requiring both high perception accuracy and low inference latency' faced by full-view world models in real-time autonomous driving.

When Should a World Model Move? Loss-Conditioned State Execution
动机: Motivation: 打破“预测准确即应行动”的固有认知，指出在绝对损失下高预测置信度并不总是对应最优控制策略，需解决盲目试错问题。Breaks the inherent cognition that 'accurate prediction implies action', pointing out that high prediction confidence under absolute loss does not always correspond to optimal control strategies, requiring the resolution of blind trial-and-error issues.
方法: Method: 提出Loss-Conditioned State Execution机制，通过校准数据过滤提案，仅当预测能显著降低损失时才执行状态更新，否则保持静止。Proposes a Loss-Conditioned State Execution mechanism, filtering proposals through data calibration, executing state updates only when predictions significantly reduce loss, otherwise remaining stationary.

动机: Motivation: 针对自动驾驶世界模型因运行完整视频生成循环而导致的算力浪费与规划延迟问题，寻求更高效的隐式规划路径。Addressing the computational waste and planning latency caused by autonomous driving world models running complete video generation loops, this work seeks more efficient implicit planning paths.
方法: Method: 在Wan2.2骨干网络上设计“多出口”机制（Adaptive-WAM），根据中间视频扩散特征的质量动态决定规划终止时机。We design an "multi-exit" mechanism (Adaptive-WAM) on the Wan2.2 backbone network, dynamically determining the planning termination timing based on the quality of intermediate video diffusion features.
推进: Advance: 实现了10%-47%的算力节省，验证了基于中间特征进行早期退出规划的可行性，为长视野推理提供了低延迟的优化方案。We achieve 10%-47% computational savings, validating the feasibility of early-exit planning based on intermediate features and providing a low-latency optimization solution for long-horizon reasoning.

动机: Motivation: 针对世界模型潜空间正则化虽利于自监督学习却未必提升规划性能的问题，亟需建立专门评估规划相关质量的指标。Addressing the issue that latent space regularization in World Models, while beneficial for self-supervised learning, does not necessarily improve planning performance, necessitating the establishment of metrics specifically evaluating planning-related quality.

动机: Motivation: 揭示长程规划失败并非源于预测器精度不足，而是规划器无法有效利用世界模型潜空间中的几何信息。Revealing that long-horizon planning failures do not stem from insufficient predictor accuracy, but rather from the planner's inability to effectively utilize geometric information within the latent space of the world model.
方法: Method: 通过复现与诊断发现，规划器使用的欧氏距离目标函数在长距离下存在饱和与反转问题，导致无法利用潜空间中的位置编码。Through reproduction and diagnosis, it was found that the Euclidean distance objective function used by planners suffers from saturation and reversal issues over long distances, leading to an inability to utilize position encoding in the latent space.
推进: Advance: 证明仅替换规划目标函数即可将长程成功率从26%提升至98%，指出隐式规划的核心瓶颈在于规划几何结构而非预测精度。Demonstrating that merely replacing the planning objective function can increase long-horizon success rates from 26% to 98%, pointing out that the core bottleneck of implicit planning lies in planning geometric structure rather than prediction accuracy.

Reinforced Planning with Latent World Models
动机: Motivation: 单纯依赖世界模型预测未来不足以支撑复杂规划，若缺乏对“如何改进计划”的学习，模型极易受幻觉影响导致决策崩溃。Simply relying on world model predictions of the future is insufficient to support complex planning; without learning 'how to improve the plan', models are highly susceptible to hallucinations leading to decision collapse.

Towards Zero-Shot Task Transfer with Neurosymbolic World Models

方法: Method: 构建神经符号世界模型，将奖励预测从黑盒潜变量中解耦，映射到可解释的符号属性上以实现零样本任务适应。Constructing a neuro-symbolic world model that decouples reward prediction from black-box latent variables, mapping them to interpretable symbolic attributes to achieve zero-shot task adaptation.

动机: Motivation: 为解决单一潜空间在长视界规划中易发生距离坍缩及递归误差累积的问题，试图通过解耦局部执行与长期规划来提升稳定性。To address the issues of distance collapse and recursive error accumulation in single latent spaces during long-horizon planning, it attempts to improve stability by decoupling local execution from long-term planning.
方法: Method: 构建双潜空间世界模型（Dual-WM），分离局部与长期表征，并引入LoRe加权rollout机制与MAPS正则化来抑制误差传播。It constructs a Dual-WM (Dual World Model), separating local and long-term representations, and introducing a LoRe weighted rollout mechanism and MAPS regularization to suppress error propagation.
推进: Advance: 证明了双空间架构能有效缓解长视界任务中的特征坍塌，在离线数据上实现了比LeWM更稳健的规划性能，但也暴露了模型复杂度增加与泛化性存疑的工程代价。It proves that the dual-space architecture can effectively alleviate feature collapse in long-horizon tasks, achieving more robust planning performance than LeWM on offline data, but also exposes the engineering cost of increased model complexity and questionable generalization.

Anisotropic Representations Improve Planning in JEPA World Models
动机: Motivation: 批判性地指出JEPA架构中各向同性正则化虽能防止特征坍塌，却会导致规划空间几何扭曲，使得预测准确不等于规划有效。It critically points out that while isotropic regularization in JEPA architectures prevents feature collapse, it leads to geometric distortion in the planning space, making prediction accuracy not equivalent to planning effectiveness.
方法: Method: 提出各向异性表示（Anisotropic Representations），通过引入可学习的各向异性目标函数修正潜空间中的几何错配，替代传统的SIGReg正则化。It proposes Anisotropic Representations, introducing learnable anisotropic objective functions to correct geometric mismatches in latent spaces, replacing traditional SIGReg regularization.
推进: Advance: 揭示了规划性能不仅取决于预测精度，更依赖于潜空间几何结构的保真度，证明了微调正则化项即可在特定环境中提升成功率，但同时也暴露了该方法对超参数敏感且难以解决架构本质的局限。It reveals that planning performance depends not only on prediction accuracy but more on the fidelity of the latent space geometric structure, proving that fine-tuning regularization terms can improve success rates in specific environments, but also exposes the method's sensitivity to hyperparameters and its inability to resolve fundamental architectural limitations.

Do-JEPA: From Masking to Intervention in Latent World Models
动机: Motivation: 现有隐式规划模型多依赖相关性预测，难以区分动作的因果效应与观测噪声，导致在复杂物理环境中规划缺乏可解释性与鲁棒性。Existing implicit planning models mostly rely on correlation prediction, making it difficult to distinguish causal effects of actions from observation noise, leading to a lack of interpretability and robustness in planning within complex physical environments.
方法: Method: 提出 Do-JEPA 架构，将传统的掩码预测升级为基于模拟器的物理干预机制，通过对比干预前后的潜在状态变化来显式学习因果图。Proposing the Do-JEPA architecture, which upgrades traditional masked prediction to a simulator-based physical intervention mechanism, explicitly learning causal graphs by comparing latent state changes before and after intervention.
推进: Advance: 成功从相关性表征中解耦出因果效应，证明了在潜在空间进行物理干预比单纯像素预测更能精准定位动作入口，为构建无需显式试错的长视野推理提供了因果基础。Successfully decoupling causal effects from correlation representations, proving that physical intervention in latent space is more precise in locating action entry points than simple pixel prediction, providing a causal foundation for long-horizon reasoning without explicit trial-and-error.

空间感知与物理常识Spatial Perception and Physical Common Sense

该方向聚焦于解决具身智能在空间感知与物理常识上的核心短板，即模型缺乏对 3D 几何结构、拓扑关系及物理动力学的深层理解。演进脉络从早期依赖大规模合成数据（如 SceneVerse）提升语义理解，转向构建显式 3D 记忆（3D-Mem）与认知地图以增强空间推理。近期研究更强调物理约束的显式注入，通过几何流形（RelAfford6D）、时空网格引导（G2DP）及反事实物理测试（PhysBench, Testing Frontier）来纠正模型的“幻觉”。核心思路正从单纯的视觉语义匹配，向“几何 + 物理 + 推理”的深度融合转变，旨在让机器人真正“懂”物理世界而非仅“看”到物体。This direction focuses on addressing the core weaknesses of embodied AI in spatial perception and physical common sense, namely the model's lack of deep understanding of 3D geometric structures, topological relationships, and physical dynamics. The evolutionary trajectory has shifted from early reliance on large-scale synthetic data (e.g., SceneVerse) to improve semantic understanding, towards constructing explicit 3D memory (3D-Mem) and cognitive maps to enhance spatial reasoning. Recent research emphasizes the explicit injection of physical constraints, using geometric manifolds (RelAfford6D), spatiotemporal grid guidance (G2DP), and counterfactual physical tests (PhysBench, Testing Frontier) to correct model 'hallucinations'. The core approach is shifting from simple visual-semantic matching towards a deep fusion of 'geometry + physics + reasoning', aiming to enable robots to truly 'understand' the physical world rather than merely 'seeing' objects.

方法: Method: 在JEPA框架中显式注入逆动力学（IDM）和状态对齐（SA）约束，强制模型在预测未来状态时遵循物理定律。Explicit injection of Inverse Dynamics (IDM) and State Alignment (SA) constraints into the JEPA framework forces the model to follow physical laws when predicting future states.
推进: Advance: 揭示了单纯依赖“时间直线化”指标可能导致虚假繁荣，证明了物理接地（Physical Grounding）能显著提升复杂操作任务的成功率与表征真实性。It reveals that relying solely on the 'time linearization' metric may lead to false prosperity, proving that physical grounding can significantly improve the success rate and representational authenticity of complex manipulation tasks.

方法: Method: 提出谱技能（Spectral Skills）表示法，利用谱方向分解运动模式，实现不同动作基元的零样本组合与未来状态预测。Propose Spectral Skills representation, utilizing spectral direction decomposition of motion patterns to achieve zero-shot composition of different action primitives and future state prediction.
推进: Advance: 展示了从“重建过去”转向“预测未来”的表征范式能显著提升运动组合的灵活性，但指出其泛化能力受限于离线数据对物理动作空间的覆盖度。Showed that shifting the representation paradigm from 'reconstructing the past' to 'predicting the future' can significantly enhance the flexibility of motion composition, but pointed out that its generalization ability is limited by the coverage of offline data on the physical action space.

Towards Spatial Perception for Heterogeneous Robot Collaboration in Subterranean Mining Environments
动机: Motivation: 针对地下采矿等极端环境下机器人面临的高带宽限制与复杂非结构化地形，解决传统点云传输不可行及视觉感知在低光照、高粉尘中失效的难题。Addressing the challenges of high bandwidth limitations and complex unstructured terrains faced by robots in extreme environments such as underground mining, solving the infeasibility of traditional point cloud transmission and the failure of visual perception in low-light and high-dust conditions.
方法: Method: 提出双机协作架构，利用零样本视觉语言分割提取语义信息，并将高维点云压缩为带法向量的多边形几何摘要进行传输。Proposing a dual-robot collaboration architecture that utilizes zero-shot visual language segmentation to extract semantic information and compresses high-dimensional point clouds into polygonal geometric summaries with normals for transmission.
推进: Advance: 实现了在带宽受限且视觉条件恶劣的真实场景中，将复杂的地质结构简化为可推理的几何拓扑，显著提升了异构机器人间的空间信息交互效率。Achieving the simplification of complex geological structures into reasoning-capable geometric topologies in real-world scenarios with bandwidth constraints and poor visual conditions, significantly improving spatial information interaction efficiency between heterogeneous robots.

数据驱动与仿真基准Data-driven and Simulation Benchmarks

该方向致力于通过大规模合成数据与自动化流水线解决具身智能中数据稀缺与长程规划难题。核心思路是从“人工标注”转向“生成式合成”，利用LLM和扩散模型构建高保真仿真环境（如RoboCasa, AnyPlace）及复杂任务基准（如VLABench, SafeAgentBench）。演进路径从单一技能训练转向长程推理与安全性评测，通过引入多模态记忆、流式导航及物理优化加速（如GPU IPOPT），推动模型从“仿真刷榜”向“真实世界泛化”跨越，重点解决VLA模型在常识推理、安全约束及长时序任务中的“最后一公里”鸿沟。This direction aims to solve data scarcity and long-horizon planning challenges in embodied AI through large-scale synthetic data and automated pipelines. The core idea shifts from 'manual annotation' to 'generative synthesis', utilizing LLMs and diffusion models to construct high-fidelity simulation environments (e.g., RoboCasa, AnyPlace) and complex task benchmarks (e.g., VLABench, SafeAgentBench). The evolutionary path moves from single-skill training to long-horizon reasoning and safety evaluation, promoting the transition of models from 'simulation benchmarking' to 'real-world generalization' by introducing multimodal memory, streaming navigation, and physics optimization acceleration (e.g., GPU IPOPT), focusing on bridging the 'last mile' gap for VLA models in commonsense reasoning, safety constraints, and long-horizon tasks.

动机: Motivation: 为解决世界模型在具身智能中难以剥离视觉噪声、单纯评估长程状态推理与记忆维护能力的难题，需要构建轻量级且纯粹的动态预测基准。To address the difficulty of isolating visual noise in world models for embodied intelligence and purely evaluating long-horizon state reasoning and memory maintenance capabilities, a lightweight and pure dynamic prediction benchmark is needed.

推进: Advance: 证明了显式的持久化空间数据结构对语言接地和状态预测的关键作用，为诊断LLM在长时序任务中的内部状态维护短板提供了可复现的纯推理基准。It proves the critical role of explicit persistent spatial data structures in language grounding and state prediction, providing a reproducible pure reasoning benchmark for diagnosing LLM shortcomings in maintaining internal states during long-horizon tasks.

推进: Advance: 通过显式建模时空依赖关系，显著提升了动态环境下的负载均衡能力，为仿真环境中的多智能体协同规划提供了新的动态特征提取范式。By explicitly modeling spatiotemporal dependency relationships, load balancing capabilities in dynamic environments are significantly improved, providing a new dynamic feature extraction paradigm for multi-agent collaborative planning in simulation environments.

From Proxy Learning to Driving Decisions: A Transfer-Based Framework for Evaluating Future-Aware Autonomous Driving Planners
动机: Motivation: 揭示当前具身规划研究中普遍存在的“代理指标（如预测误差）提升不等于实际决策能力增强”的评估幻觉，亟需建立从感知到决策的因果验证基准。It reveals the evaluation illusion prevalent in current embodied planning research that 'improvement in proxy metrics (e.g., prediction error) does not equate to enhanced actual decision-making capability', urgently necessitating the establishment of a causal verification benchmark from perception to decision.
方法: Method: 构建PDT（Proxy to Decision Transfer）评估框架，在NAVSIM-v1等基准上拆解预测准确性与最终决策安全性的映射链条，进行深度尸检分析。The PDT (Proxy to Decision Transfer) evaluation framework is constructed, decomposing the mapping chain between prediction accuracy and final decision safety on benchmarks such as NAVSIM-v1 for deep autopsy analysis.
推进: Advance: 实证发现大量基于代理指标优化的规划器在完整任务中安全指标反而倒退，确立了以决策结果为导向的仿真评测新标准，遏制了“注水”论文。Empirical findings reveal that many planners optimized based on proxy metrics actually regress in safety metrics during complete tasks, establishing a new standard for simulation evaluation oriented towards decision results and curbing 'watered-down' papers.

方法: Method: 采用认证符号规划方法，将高维动力学简化为操作空间双积分器模型，并在离散格点上执行符号控制综合以生成安全轨迹。Adopted certified symbolic planning methods to simplify high-dimensional dynamics into a double-integrator model in operational space, and executed symbolic control synthesis on discrete grids to generate safe trajectories.
推进: Advance: 首次在Franka FR3硬件上实现了闭环控制与形式化验证的结合，证明了在仿真与真实世界间跨越“安全鸿沟”的可行性，弥补了纯数据驱动方法的安全短板。For the first time, achieved the combination of closed-loop control and formal verification on Franka FR3 hardware, proving the feasibility of bridging the 'safety gap' between simulation and the real world, andaddressing the safety shortcomings of purely data-driven methods.

Retrieved but not ranked: surface-form bias in structural retrieval, from mathematics to agent trajectories
动机: Motivation: 揭露当前基于语义检索的规划基准存在严重的表面形式偏差，导致模型在长程推理任务中依赖关键词匹配而非真正的结构理解。Exposed severe superficial form bias in current semantic retrieval-based planning benchmarks, causing models to rely on keyword matching rather than true structural understanding in long-horizon reasoning tasks.
方法: Method: 通过数学竞赛与具身轨迹的对比实验，利用词法重排序作为诊断工具，量化分析检索质量对求解器性能的实际影响及上下文截断误差。Through comparative experiments on mathematical competitions and embodied trajectories, utilized lexical reordering as a diagnostic tool to quantitatively analyze the actual impact of retrieval quality on solver performance and context truncation errors.
推进: Advance: 证实了现有基准测试构建方式决定了评估结果是“对抗性”还是“偶然性”，指出单纯依赖检索增强无法解决长时序任务中的推理鸿沟，需重构评估逻辑。Confirmed that the construction method of existing benchmarks determines whether evaluation results are 'adversarial' or 'accidental', pointing out that relying solely on retrieval augmentation cannot solve the reasoning gap in long-horizon tasks, requiring a reconstruction of evaluation logic.

推进: Advance: 揭示了模型在长程时空推理上的“虚胖”真相，证明了合成数据在评估特定场景下模型真实短板的有效性，尽管存在地理多样性不足的问题。Reveals the truth of 'bloating' in models' long-range spatiotemporal reasoning, proving the effectiveness of synthetic data in evaluating specific model weaknesses in certain scenarios, despite issues of insufficient geographical diversity.

推进: Advance: 指出了数据驱动仿真中单纯依赖自动化验证器可能导致Reward Hacking的致命缺陷，为构建更鲁棒的长程规划评测体系提供了理论警示。Points out the fatal flaw that relying solely on automated verifiers in data-driven simulation may lead to Reward Hacking, providing a theoretical warning for building a more robust long-range planning evaluation system.

推进: Advance: 通过技能复用机制在LIBERO-10上大幅提升首试成功率，验证了从单一技能训练向具备记忆与进化能力的长程推理系统演进的有效性。Significantly improved first-attempt success rates on LIBERO-10 through skill reuse mechanisms, validating the effectiveness of evolving from single-skill training to long-horizon reasoning systems with memory and evolutionary capabilities.

系统架构与理论框架System Architecture and Theoretical Framework

在系统架构与理论框架视角下，Planning & Reasoning 正从单一智能体向分布式、认知基础设施演进。核心思路是引入 Holonic 数字孪生、经济世界模型及 ISAC 闭环，将物理 AI 的因果推理与不确定性处理提升至网络与认知层面。演进路径表现为：从被动镜像转向主动代理人（#1），构建分层经济仿真以规范智能体行为（#5），并强制打通感知通信与决策的闭环验证（#8）。尽管当前多处于理论蓝图阶段，缺乏实证，但这些框架为解决长视界不确定性与跨域协同提供了必要的数学与系统论基础。From the perspective of system architecture and theoretical framework, Planning & Reasoning is evolving from single agents to distributed cognitive infrastructure. The core idea is to introduce Holonic digital twins, economic world models, and ISAC closed loops to elevate the causal reasoning and uncertainty handling of physical AI to the network and cognitive levels. The evolutionary path manifests as: shifting from passive mirroring to active agents (#1), constructing hierarchical economic simulations to regulate agent behavior (#5), and mandating theintegration of closed-loop validation between perception-communication and decision-making (#8). Although currently mostly in the theoretical blueprint stage and lacking empirical evidence, these frameworks provide the necessary mathematical and systems theory foundations for addressing long-horizon uncertainty and cross-domain collaboration.

Motion-as-Prompt: Enhancing Motion Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models via Motion-Guided Cross-Frame Visual Prompting
动机: Motivation: 解决多模态大模型（MLLM）因稀疏采样导致长视界运动推理失效及物体“瞬移”等因果断裂问题，无需重构模型架构即可提升运动理解。Solving the failure of long-horizon motion reasoning and causal breaks such as object 'teleportation' in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) caused by sparse sampling, improving motion understanding without reconstructing the model architecture.
方法: Method: 提出 Motion-as-Prompt (MaP) 框架，利用冻结的外部点追踪器（如 CoTracker3）提取关键帧轨迹，将其作为视觉提示注入 MLLM 输入端。Proposing the Motion-as-Prompt (MaP) framework, which utilizes a frozen external point tracker (e.g., CoTracker3) to extract keyframe trajectories and injects them as visual prompts into the MLLM input.

动机: Motivation: 针对云端推理架构中网络延迟导致物理控制失效的痛点，需在系统层面解决感知通信与决策执行之间的时序错配问题。Addressing the pain point of physical control failure caused by network latency in cloud inference architectures, it is necessary to solve the temporal mismatch between perception communication and decision execution at the system level.

An Omitted Mode Is a Rare Rule: The Sampling-Verification Danger Law in Continuous Code World Models
动机: Motivation: 揭示连续控制世界中“采样验证即安全”的数学盲区，指出光滑模型无法通过采样覆盖所有罕见规则，导致规划器在部署时面临致命风险。We reveal the mathematical blind spot of 'sampling verification implies safety' in continuous control worlds, pointing out that smooth models cannot cover all rare rules via sampling, leading to fatal risks for planners during deployment.
方法: Method: 建立采样 - 验证危险定律，引入“定位预算”概念，从数学上证明连续域中错误压缩的边界条件，区分离散与连续世界的验证差异。We establish the Sampling-Verification Danger Law, introduce the concept of 'localization budget,' and mathematically prove the boundary conditions for error compression in continuous domains, distinguishing verification differences between discrete and continuous worlds.
推进: Advance: 打破了Code World Model盲目依赖采样覆盖率的迷思，为长视界不确定性下的系统架构提供了严格的理论边界，警示了纯数据驱动验证的脆弱性。We break the myth of Code World Model blindly relying on sampling coverage, providing strict theoretical boundaries for system architectures under long-horizon uncertainty and warning of the fragility of purely data-driven verification.

CompCPZ: Preserving Multi-Modal Intent in Language-Guided Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决语言引导机器人操作中因语义模糊（如“或”逻辑）导致拓扑结构丢失、意图表达失真的问题，防止模型因代数缺陷产生错误规划。We address the problem of lost topological structures and distorted intent expression in language-guided robot manipulation due to semantic ambiguity (e.g., 'OR' logic), preventing erroneous planning caused by algebraic defects in models.
方法: Method: 构建CompCPZ代数层，利用多项式 zonotope 显式表示多模态意图集合，强制保留逻辑运算（或、非）的连通分量结构。We construct the CompCPZ algebraic layer, utilizing polynomial zonotopes to explicitly represent multimodal intent sets, thereby enforcing the connectivity component structure of logical operations (OR, NOT).
推进: Advance: 证明了少于k个连通分量的编码必然丢失模式间距，从系统论层面修复了VLA模型的拓扑缺陷，为多模态意图的保真传输提供了数学基础。We prove that encoding with fewer than k connectivity components inevitably loses pattern spacing, repairing the topological defects of VLA models from a systems theory perspective and providing a mathematical foundation for faithful transmission of multimodal intents.
